- [ ] If this happened during a chat session, I included as much full session context as possible and redacted secrets

### What happened?

Running the official install command fails because the installer tries to download a `.deb` file that returns HTTP 404.

### Expected behavior

The installer should successfully download the ARM64 .deb package and continue the installation.
OS: Linux
Architecture: aarch64
Command: uname -m
Output: aarch64

### Steps to reproduce

Steps to reproduce the behavior:

1. Run the install script:
   ```bash
   curl -fsSL https://www.moltis.org/install.sh | sh

author:	cedhuf
association:	none
edited:	false
status:	none
--
Hi there,

I ran into the same issue while trying to **update Moltis on an amd64 system** using the official `install.sh` script. The problem comes from a mismatch between the script’s expected `.deb` filename and what’s actually published in the release.

The `install.sh` script tries to download something like: moltis_20260327.05-1_amd64.deb
…but the actual release asset on GitHub is: moltis_20260327.05_amd64.deb

Notice there is **no `-1` suffix**. This is why the script fails with 404 errors or downloads a tiny, invalid file.

---

### How to update manually

1. Make sure you are using the **correct architecture** — in this case `amd64`. Other releases (like ARM) have different `.deb` names.  

2. Download the proper `.deb` for amd64:

```
curl -fL -o moltis_20260327.05_amd64.deb \
  https://github.com/moltis-org/moltis/releases/download/20260327.05/moltis_20260327.05_amd64.deb
```

3.	Install it:

`sudo dpkg -i moltis_20260327.05_amd64.deb`

4.	If any dependencies are missing, fix them:

`sudo apt-get install -f`

5.	Verify:

```
moltis --version
# Should output: 20260327.05
```
⸻

Key points
	•	Always check the release assets for your architecture. Using latest or the install script blindly can fail because it assumes a -1 suffix that may not exist.
	•	The install.sh script essentially just fetches the .deb for your architecture and installs it — it doesn’t do anything beyond that.

This approach worked for me to safely update Moltis on amd64 without breaking the installation.
--
```

   101→detect_os() {
   102→    OS="$(uname -s)"
   103→    case "$OS" in
   104→        Darwin)
   105→            echo "macos"
   106→            ;;
   107→        Linux)
   108→            echo "linux"
   109→            ;;
   110→        MINGW*|MSYS*|CYGWIN*)
   111→            echo "windows"
   112→            ;;
   113→        *)
   114→            echo "unknown"
   115→            ;;
   116→    esac
   117→}
   118→
   119→detect_arch() {
   120→    ARCH="$(uname -m)"
   121→    case "$ARCH" in
   122→        x86_64|amd64)
   123→            echo "x86_64"
   124→            ;;
   125→        aarch64|arm64)
   126→            echo "aarch64"
   127→            ;;
   128→        armv7l)
   129→            echo "armv7"
   130→            ;;
   131→        i386|i686)
   132→            echo "i686"
   133→            ;;
   134→        *)
   135→            echo "$ARCH"
   136→            ;;
   137→    esac
   138→}
   139→
   140→detect_linux_distro() {
   141→    if [ -f /etc/os-release ]; then
   142→        # shellcheck disable=SC1091
   143→        . /etc/os-release
   144→        echo "$ID"
   145→    elif [ -f /etc/debian_version ]; then
   146→        echo "debian"
   147→    elif [ -f /etc/redhat-release ]; then
   148→        echo "rhel"
   149→    elif [ -f /etc/arch-release ]; then
   150→        echo "arch"
   151→    else
   152→        echo "unknown"
   153→    fi
   154→}
   155→
   156→command_exists() {
   157→    command -v "$1" >/dev/null 2>&1
   158→}
   159→
   160→get_latest_version() {
   161→    # Extract tag_name, stripping optional leading "v" prefix.
   162→    if command_exists curl; then
   163→        curl -fsSL "https://api.github.com/repos/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/latest" | grep '"tag_name":' | sed -E 's/.*"tag_name": *"v?([^"]+)".*/\1/'
   164→    elif command_exists wget; then
   165→        wget -qO- "https://api.github.com/repos/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/latest" | grep '"tag_name":' | sed -E 's/.*"tag_name": *"v?([^"]+)".*/\1/'
   166→    else
   167→        error "Neither curl nor wget found. Please install one of them."
   168→    fi
   169→}
   170→
   171→# Return the GitHub release tag for a given version.
   172→# Date-based versions (YYYYMMDD.NN) are bare tags; semver gets a "v" prefix.
   173→release_tag() {
   174→    v="$1"
   175→    case "$v" in
   176→        [0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9].*)
   177→            echo "$v" ;;
   178→        *)
   179→            echo "v$v" ;;
   180→    esac
   181→}
   182→
   183→download() {
   184→    url="$1"
   185→    dest="$2"
   186→    if command_exists curl; then
   187→        curl -fsSL "$url" -o "$dest"
   188→    elif command_exists wget; then
   189→        wget -q "$url" -O "$dest"
   190→    else
   191→        error "Neither curl nor wget found. Please install one of them."
   192→    fi
   193→}
   194→
   195→verify_checksum() {
   196→    file="$1"
   197→    expected_sha256="$2"
   198→
   199→    if command_exists sha256sum; then
   200→        actual=$(sha256sum "$file" | cut -d' ' -f1)
   201→    elif command_exists shasum; then
   202→        actual=$(shasum -a 256 "$file" | cut -d' ' -f1)
   203→    else
   204→        warn "Cannot verify checksum (sha256sum/shasum not found)"
   205→        return 0
   206→    fi
   207→
   208→    if [ "$actual" != "$expected_sha256" ]; then
   209→        error "Checksum verification failed!\nExpected: $expected_sha256\nActual: $actual"
   210→    fi
   211→}
   212→
   213→ensure_install_dir() {
   214→    if [ ! -d "$INSTALL_DIR" ]; then
   215→        mkdir -p "$INSTALL_DIR"
   216→    fi
   217→}
   218→
   219→install_shared_assets() {
   220→    source_dir="$1"
   221→    if [ ! -d "$source_dir" ]; then
   222→        return 0
   223→    fi
   224→
   225→    share_dir="$HOME/.moltis/share"
   226→    mkdir -p "$share_dir"
   227→    cp -R "$source_dir"/. "$share_dir"/
   228→    info "Installed shared assets to $share_dir"
   229→}
   230→
   231→add_to_path_instructions() {
   232→    shell_name=$(basename "$SHELL")
   233→    case "$shell_name" in
   234→        bash)
   235→            rc_file="$HOME/.bashrc"
   236→            ;;
   237→        zsh)
   238→            rc_file="$HOME/.zshrc"
   239→            ;;
   240→        fish)
   241→            rc_file="$HOME/.config/fish/config.fish"
   242→            ;;
   243→        *)
   244→            rc_file="$HOME/.profile"
   245→            ;;
   246→    esac
   247→
   248→    # Check if already in PATH
   249→    case ":$PATH:" in
   250→        *":$INSTALL_DIR:"*)
   251→            return
   252→            ;;
   253→    esac
   254→
   255→    printf "\n"
   256→    warn "$INSTALL_DIR is not in your PATH."
   257→    printf "Add it by running:\n\n"
   258→    if [ "$shell_name" = "fish" ]; then
   259→        printf "  ${BOLD}fish_add_path %s${NC}\n\n" "$INSTALL_DIR"
   260→    else
   261→        printf "  ${BOLD}echo 'export PATH=\"%s:\$PATH\"' >> %s${NC}\n\n" "$INSTALL_DIR" "$rc_file"
   262→    fi
   263→    printf "Then restart your shell or run:\n"
   264→    printf "  ${BOLD}source %s${NC}\n" "$rc_file"
   265→}
   266→
   267→# Installation methods
   268→
   269→install_homebrew() {
   270→    info "Installing via Homebrew..."
   271→    if ! command_exists brew; then
   272→        error "Homebrew not found. Install it from https://brew.sh/"
   273→    fi
   274→    brew tap "$HOMEBREW_TAP" 2>/dev/null || true
   275→    brew install moltis
   276→    success "Moltis installed via Homebrew"
   277→}
   278→
   279→install_binary() {
   280→    os="$1"
   281→    arch="$2"
   282→    version="$3"
   283→
   284→    # Determine target triple
   285→    case "$os" in
   286→        macos)
   287→            target="${arch}-apple-darwin"
   288→            ;;
   289→        linux)
   290→            target="${arch}-unknown-linux-gnu"
   291→            ;;
   292→        *)
   293→            error "Unsupported OS for binary installation: $os"
   294→            ;;
   295→    esac
   296→
   297→    tag=$(release_tag "$version")
   298→    tarball="${BINARY_NAME}-${version}-${target}.tar.gz"
   299→    url="https://github.com/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/download/${tag}/${tarball}"
   300→    checksum_url="${url}.sha256"
   301→
   302→    info "Downloading ${BINARY_NAME} v${version} for ${target}..."
   303→
   304→    tmpdir=$(mktemp -d)
   305→    trap 'rm -rf "$tmpdir"' EXIT
   306→
   307→    download "$url" "$tmpdir/$tarball" || error "Failed to download $tarball. Check if a release exists for your platform."
   308→
   309→    # Verify checksum
   310→    if download "$checksum_url" "$tmpdir/checksum.sha256" 2>/dev/null; then
   311→        expected_sha=$(cut -d' ' -f1 "$tmpdir/checksum.sha256")
   312→        verify_checksum "$tmpdir/$tarball" "$expected_sha"
   313→        info "Checksum verified"
   314→    else
   315→        warn "Could not download checksum file, skipping verification"
   316→    fi
   317→
   318→    # Extract and install
   319→    tar -xzf "$tmpdir/$tarball" -C "$tmpdir"
   320→
   321→    ensure_install_dir
   322→    mv "$tmpdir/$BINARY_NAME" "$INSTALL_DIR/$BINARY_NAME"
   323→    chmod +x "$INSTALL_DIR/$BINARY_NAME"
   324→
   325→    if [ -d "$tmpdir/share/moltis" ]; then
   326→        install_shared_assets "$tmpdir/share/moltis"
   327→    elif [ -d "$tmpdir/share/web" ] && [ -d "$tmpdir/share/wasm" ]; then
   328→        install_shared_assets "$tmpdir/share"
   329→    fi
   330→
   331→    success "Moltis installed to $INSTALL_DIR/$BINARY_NAME"
   332→    add_to_path_instructions
   333→}
   334→
   335→install_deb() {
   336→    arch="$1"
   337→    version="$2"
   338→
   339→    case "$arch" in
   340→        x86_64) deb_arch="amd64" ;;
   341→        aarch64) deb_arch="arm64" ;;
   342→        *) error "Unsupported architecture for .deb: $arch" ;;
   343→    esac
   344→
   345→    # Package naming: moltis_VERSION-REV_ARCH.deb
   346→    tag=$(release_tag "$version")
   347→    deb_file="moltis_${version}-1_${deb_arch}.deb"
   348→    url="https://github.com/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/download/${tag}/${deb_file}"
   349→
   350→    info "Downloading ${deb_file}..."
   351→
   352→    tmpdir=$(mktemp -d)
   353→    trap 'rm -rf "$tmpdir"' EXIT
   354→
   355→    download "$url" "$tmpdir/$deb_file" || error "Failed to download $deb_file"
   356→
   357→    info "Installing .deb package (requires sudo)..."
   358→    sudo dpkg -i "$tmpdir/$deb_file" || sudo apt-get install -f -y
   359→
   360→    success "Moltis installed via .deb package"
   361→}
   362→
   363→install_rpm() {
   364→    arch="$1"
   365→    version="$2"
   366→
   367→    case "$arch" in
   368→        x86_64) rpm_arch="x86_64" ;;
   369→        aarch64) rpm_arch="aarch64" ;;
   370→        *) error "Unsupported architecture for .rpm: $arch" ;;
   371→    esac
   372→
   373→    # Package naming: moltis-VERSION-1.ARCH.rpm
   374→    tag=$(release_tag "$version")
   375→    rpm_file="moltis-${version}-1.${rpm_arch}.rpm"
   376→    url="https://github.com/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/download/${tag}/${rpm_file}"
   377→
   378→    info "Downloading ${rpm_file}..."
   379→
   380→    tmpdir=$(mktemp -d)
   381→    trap 'rm -rf "$tmpdir"' EXIT
   382→
   383→    download "$url" "$tmpdir/$rpm_file" || error "Failed to download $rpm_file"
   384→
   385→    info "Installing .rpm package (requires sudo)..."
   386→    if command_exists dnf; then
   387→        sudo dnf install -y "$tmpdir/$rpm_file"
   388→    elif command_exists yum; then
   389→        sudo yum install -y "$tmpdir/$rpm_file"
   390→    else
   391→        sudo rpm -i "$tmpdir/$rpm_file"
   392→    fi
   393→
   394→    success "Moltis installed via .rpm package"
   395→}
   396→
   397→install_arch() {
   398→    arch="$1"
   399→    version="$2"
   400→
   401→    tag=$(release_tag "$version")
   402→    pkg_file="moltis-${version}-1-${arch}.pkg.tar.zst"
   403→    url="https://github.com/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/download/${tag}/${pkg_file}"
   404→
   405→    info "Downloading ${pkg_file}..."
   406→
   407→    tmpdir=$(mktemp -d)
   408→    trap 'rm -rf "$tmpdir"' EXIT
   409→
   410→    download "$url" "$tmpdir/$pkg_file" || error "Failed to download $pkg_file"
   411→
   412→    info "Installing Arch package (requires sudo)..."
   413→    sudo pacman -U --noconfirm "$tmpdir/$pkg_file"
   414→
   415→    success "Moltis installed via Arch package"
   416→}
   417→
   418→install_snap() {
   419→    info "Installing via Snap..."
   420→
   421→    if ! command_exists snap; then
   422→        error "Snap not found. Install it first: https://snapcraft.io/docs/installing-snapd"
   423→    fi
   424→
   425→    sudo snap install moltis
   426→
   427→    success "Moltis installed via Snap"
   428→}
   429→
   430→install_from_source() {
   431→    warn "Building from source. This may take several minutes..."
   432→
   433→    if ! command_exists cargo; then
   434→        info "Rust not found. Installing via rustup..."
   435→        if command_exists curl; then
   436→            curl --proto '=https' --tlsv1.2 -sSf https://sh.rustup.rs | sh -s -- -y
   437→        else
   438→            wget -qO- https://sh.rustup.rs | sh -s -- -y
   439→        fi
   440→        # shellcheck disable=SC1091
   441→        . "$HOME/.cargo/env"
   442→    fi
   443→
   444→    if ! command_exists git; then
   445→        error "Git is required to build from source. Please install it first."
   446→    fi
   447→
   448→    version="$1"
   449→
   450→    tmpdir=$(mktemp -d)
   451→    trap 'rm -rf "$tmpdir"' EXIT
   452→
   453→    info "Cloning repository..."
   454→    tag=$(release_tag "$version")
   455→    git clone --depth 1 --branch "$tag" "https://github.com/${GITHUB_REPO}.git" "$tmpdir/moltis"
   456→
   457→    cd "$tmpdir/moltis"
   458→
   459→    info "Building WASM tool components..."
   460→    rustup target add wasm32-wasip2
   461→    cargo build --target wasm32-wasip2 -p moltis-wasm-calc -p moltis-wasm-web-fetch -p moltis-wasm-web-search --release
   462→
   463→    info "Building release binary..."
   464→    cargo build --release
   465→
   466→    ensure_install_dir
   467→    cp "target/release/$BINARY_NAME" "$INSTALL_DIR/$BINARY_NAME"
   468→    chmod +x "$INSTALL_DIR/$BINARY_NAME"
   469→
   470→    SHARE_STAGING="$tmpdir/moltis-share"
   471→    mkdir -p "$SHARE_STAGING/web" "$SHARE_STAGING/wasm"
   472→    cp -R "crates/web/src/assets/." "$SHARE_STAGING/web/"
   473→    cp "target/wasm32-wasip2/release/moltis_wasm_calc.wasm" "$SHARE_STAGING/wasm/"
   474→    cp "target/wasm32-wasip2/release/moltis_wasm_web_fetch.wasm" "$SHARE_STAGING/wasm/"
   475→    cp "target/wasm32-wasip2/release/moltis_wasm_web_search.wasm" "$SHARE_STAGING/wasm/"
   476→    install_shared_assets "$SHARE_STAGING"
   477→
   478→    success "Moltis built and installed to $INSTALL_DIR/$BINARY_NAME"
   479→    add_to_path_instructions
   480→}
   481→
   482→# Main installation logic
   483→
   484→main() {
   485→    printf "\n"
   486→    printf "  ${BOLD}Moltis Installer${NC}\n"
   487→    printf "  Personal AI gateway - one binary, multiple LLM providers\n"
   488→    printf "\n"
   489→
   490→    OS=$(detect_os)
   491→    ARCH=$(detect_arch)
   492→
   493→    info "Detected: $OS ($ARCH)"
   494→
   495→    if [ "$OS" = "windows" ]; then
   496→        error "Windows is not supported by this installer. Please download the binary manually from:\nhttps://github.com/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases"
   497→    fi
   498→
   499→    if [ "$OS" = "unknown" ]; then
   500→        error "Unsupported operating system: $(uname -s)"
   501→    fi
   502→
   503→    # Get version
   504→    if [ -z "$VERSION" ]; then
   505→        info "Fetching latest version..."
   506→        VERSION=$(get_latest_version)
   507→        if [ -z "$VERSION" ]; then
   508→            error "Failed to determine latest version"
   509→        fi
   510→    fi
   511→    info "Version: $VERSION"
   512→
   513→    # Determine installation method
   514→    if [ -n "$PREFERRED_METHOD" ]; then
   515→        case "$PREFERRED_METHOD" in
   516→            homebrew)
   517→                install_homebrew
   518→                ;;
   519→            binary)
   520→                install_binary "$OS" "$ARCH" "$VERSION"
   521→                ;;
   522→            deb)
   523→                install_deb "$ARCH" "$VERSION"
   524→                ;;
   525→            rpm)
   526→                install_rpm "$ARCH" "$VERSION"
   527→                ;;
   528→            arch)
   529→                install_arch "$ARCH" "$VERSION"
   530→                ;;
   531→            snap)
   532→                install_snap
   533→                ;;
   534→            source)
   535→                install_from_source "$VERSION"
   536→                ;;
   537→            *)
   538→                error "Unknown installation method: $PREFERRED_METHOD"
   539→                ;;
   540→        esac
   541→    elif [ "$OS" = "macos" ]; then
   542→        # macOS: prefer Homebrew, fall back to binary
   543→        if [ "$USE_HOMEBREW" = true ] && command_exists brew; then
   544→            install_homebrew
   545→        else
   546→            install_binary "$OS" "$ARCH" "$VERSION"
   547→        fi
   548→    elif [ "$OS" = "linux" ]; then
   549→        # Linux: detect distro and use appropriate package manager
   550→        DISTRO=$(detect_linux_distro)
   551→
   552→        case "$DISTRO" in
   553→            ubuntu|debian|linuxmint|pop|elementary|zorin)
   554→                if command_exists apt-get; then
   555→                    install_deb "$ARCH" "$VERSION"
   556→                else
   557→                    install_binary "$OS" "$ARCH" "$VERSION"
   558→                fi
   559→                ;;
   560→            fedora|rhel|centos|rocky|alma|ol)
   561→                if command_exists dnf || command_exists yum; then
   562→                    install_rpm "$ARCH" "$VERSION"
   563→                else
   564→                    install_binary "$OS" "$ARCH" "$VERSION"
   565→                fi
   566→                ;;
   567→            arch|manjaro|endeavouros|garuda)
   568→                if command_exists pacman; then
   569→                    install_arch "$ARCH" "$VERSION"
   570→                else
   571→                    install_binary "$OS" "$ARCH" "$VERSION"
   572→                fi
   573→                ;;
   574→            *)
   575→                # Unknown distro: try binary, offer source as fallback
   576→                if [ "$ARCH" = "x86_64" ] || [ "$ARCH" = "aarch64" ]; then
   577→                    install_binary "$OS" "$ARCH" "$VERSION"
   578→                else
   579→                    warn "No pre-built binary available for $ARCH architecture."
   580→                    info "Falling back to building from source..."
   581→                    install_from_source "$VERSION"
   582→                fi
   583→                ;;
   584→        esac
   585→    fi
   586→
   587→    # Verify installation
   588→    if command_exists "$BINARY_NAME"; then
   589→        installed_version=$("$BINARY_NAME" --version 2>/dev/null | head -1 || echo "unknown")
   590→        printf "\n"
   591→        success "Installation complete!"
   592→        printf "  ${BOLD}%s${NC}\n" "$installed_version"
   593→        printf "\n"
   594→        printf "Get started:\n"
   595→        printf "  ${BOLD}moltis${NC}          # Start the gateway\n"
   596→        printf "  ${BOLD}moltis --help${NC}   # Show help\n"
   597→        printf "\n"
   598→        printf "Documentation: ${BLUE}https://www.moltis.org/${NC}\n"
   599→    elif [ -x "$INSTALL_DIR/$BINARY_NAME" ]; then
   600→        printf "\n"
   601→        success "Installation complete!"
   602→        printf "\n"
   603→        add_to_path_instructions
   604→    fi
   605→}
   606→
   607→main
   608→
```

commit 1dce89f411645b009166bd6e54dd734fe85b572f
Author: Fabien Penso <gpg@pen.so>
Date:   Fri Mar 27 18:36:03 2026 +0000

    fix(install): support date-based version tags in installer and package builds
    
    The install script assumed all tags had a "v" prefix and the sed regex
    failed to extract bare date-based tags like "20260327.01", producing
    malformed download URLs. Also, cargo-deb and cargo-generate-rpm used
    Cargo.toml's 0.1.0 instead of the release version for package names.
    
    Fixes #493

commit 1dce89f411645b009166bd6e54dd734fe85b572f
Author: Fabien Penso <gpg@pen.so>
Date:   Fri Mar 27 18:36:03 2026 +0000

    fix(install): support date-based version tags in installer and package builds
    
    The install script assumed all tags had a "v" prefix and the sed regex
    failed to extract bare date-based tags like "20260327.01", producing
    malformed download URLs. Also, cargo-deb and cargo-generate-rpm used
    Cargo.toml's 0.1.0 instead of the release version for package names.
    
    Fixes #493
    
    Entire-Checkpoint: 07cd76e4941e

diff --git a/install.sh b/install.sh
index c3f03966..04d0999b 100755
--- a/install.sh
+++ b/install.sh
@@ -158,15 +158,28 @@ command_exists() {
 }
 
 get_latest_version() {
+    # Extract tag_name, stripping optional leading "v" prefix.
     if command_exists curl; then
-        curl -fsSL "https://api.github.com/repos/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/latest" | grep '"tag_name":' | sed -E 's/.*"v([^"]+)".*/\1/'
+        curl -fsSL "https://api.github.com/repos/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/latest" | grep '"tag_name":' | sed -E 's/.*"tag_name": *"v?([^"]+)".*/\1/'
     elif command_exists wget; then
-        wget -qO- "https://api.github.com/repos/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/latest" | grep '"tag_name":' | sed -E 's/.*"v([^"]+)".*/\1/'
+        wget -qO- "https://api.github.com/repos/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/latest" | grep '"tag_name":' | sed -E 's/.*"tag_name": *"v?([^"]+)".*/\1/'
     else
         error "Neither curl nor wget found. Please install one of them."
     fi
 }
 
+# Return the GitHub release tag for a given version.
+# Date-based versions (YYYYMMDD.NN) are bare tags; semver gets a "v" prefix.
+release_tag() {
+    v="$1"
+    case "$v" in
+        [0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9][0-9].*)
+            echo "$v" ;;
+        *)
+            echo "v$v" ;;
+    esac
+}
+
 download() {
     url="$1"
     dest="$2"
@@ -281,8 +294,9 @@ install_binary() {
             ;;
     esac
 
+    tag=$(release_tag "$version")
     tarball="${BINARY_NAME}-${version}-${target}.tar.gz"
-    url="https://github.com/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/download/v${version}/${tarball}"
+    url="https://github.com/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/download/${tag}/${tarball}"
     checksum_url="${url}.sha256"
 
     info "Downloading ${BINARY_NAME} v${version} for ${target}..."
@@ -329,8 +343,9 @@ install_deb() {
     esac
 
     # Package naming: moltis_VERSION-REV_ARCH.deb
+    tag=$(release_tag "$version")
     deb_file="moltis_${version}-1_${deb_arch}.deb"
-    url="https://github.com/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/download/v${version}/${deb_file}"
+    url="https://github.com/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/download/${tag}/${deb_file}"
 
     info "Downloading ${deb_file}..."
 
@@ -356,8 +371,9 @@ install_rpm() {
     esac
 
     # Package naming: moltis-VERSION-1.ARCH.rpm
+    tag=$(release_tag "$version")
     rpm_file="moltis-${version}-1.${rpm_arch}.rpm"
-    url="https://github.com/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/download/v${version}/${rpm_file}"
+    url="https://github.com/${GITHUB_REPO}/releases/download/${tag}/${rpm_file}"
```

> AGENT

There's the answer. Line 334: `cargo deb ... --deb-version "$MOLTIS_VERSION"` produces `moltis_20260327.05_arm64.deb` (no `-1` revision). But the install script on line 347 hardcodes `-1` in the filename.

The RPM and Arch packages DO have `-1` in their actual filenames (confirmed from the asset list), so those are fine. Only the `.deb` naming is wrong.

> AGENT

Both files are identical. The `.polyphony/` copies are untracked and not part of the repo.

Here's the issue: the `install_deb()` function in `install.sh` constructs the filename as `moltis_${version}-1_${deb_arch}.deb` but `cargo deb --deb-version "$MOLTIS_VERSION"` (in the release workflow) produces `moltis_${version}_${deb_arch}.deb` without the `-1` revision suffix. The RPM and Arch packages do include `-1` because that's how `cargo-generate-rpm` and the Arch packaging work, so those are correct.

The fix removes the `-1` from the `.deb` filename in both `install.sh` and `website/install.sh`. Want me to commit, push, and close #496?

> AGENT

Good. Now I have the full picture of how each package type is named:

| Package | Release workflow produces | install.sh expects |
|---------|------------------------|--------------------|
| tarball | `moltis-${VERSION}-${TARGET}.tar.gz` | `moltis-${version}-${target}.tar.gz` (correct) |
| deb | `moltis_${VERSION}_${ARCH}.deb` (cargo-deb `--deb-version`) | was `-1`, now fixed |
| rpm | `moltis-${VERSION}-1.${ARCH}.rpm` (cargo-generate-rpm) | `moltis-${version}-1.${rpm_arch}.rpm` (correct) |
| arch | `moltis-${VERSION}-1-${ARCH}.pkg.tar.zst` (manual tar) | `moltis-${version}-1-${arch}.pkg.tar.zst` (correct) |

{
  "file_path": "/Users/penso/tmp/molt/moltis/scripts/check-install-package-names.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Verifies that install.sh generates download filenames matching the release\n# workflow's actual output for every package type and architecture.\n#\n# This catches drift between the installer and CI packaging (e.g. the -1\n# revision suffix that cargo-deb does not emit).\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nREPO_ROOT=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")/..\" && pwd)\"\nINSTALL_SH=\"$REPO_ROOT/install.sh\"\n\nif [[ ! -f \"$INSTALL_SH\" ]]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: install.sh not found at $INSTALL_SH\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\nFAILURES=0\nCHECKS=0\n\nfail() {\n  echo \"FAIL: $1\" >&2\n  FAILURES=$((FAILURES + 1))\n}\n\npass() {\n  echo \"  ok: $1\"\n  CHECKS=$((CHECKS + 1))\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Source the functions from install.sh in a subshell-safe way.\n# We override info/warn/error/success to be silent and extract\n# just the filename-building logic.\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\n# Helper: extract the expected filename from install.sh for a given function.\n# We source install.sh, override helpers, call the target function with test\n# args, and capture the download URL it would construct.\nextract_filename() {\n  local func=\"$1\"\n  shift\n  # Run in a subshell to avoid polluting our env.\n  # We override download() to just echo the url so we can capture the filename.\n  (\n    info()    { :; }\n    success() { :; }\n    warn()    { :; }\n    error()   { echo \"ERROR: $1\" >&2; exit 1; }\n    download() { echo \"URL=$1\"; exit 0; }\n    sudo()    { echo \"URL=dpkg:$2\"; exit 0; }  # intercept sudo dpkg -i\n    # Source install.sh to get all functions\n    # shellcheck disable=SC1090\n    . \"$INSTALL_SH\"\n    \"$func\" \"$@\" 2>/dev/null || true\n  ) 2>/dev/null | grep -oP '(?<=URL=)[^\\s]+' | head -1\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Expected patterns from release.yml (the source of truth):\n#\n#   deb:     moltis_${VERSION}_${ARCH}.deb\n#            (cargo deb --deb-version \"$VERSION\" — no revision suffix)\n#\n#   rpm:     moltis-${VERSION}-1.${ARCH}.rpm\n#            (cargo generate-rpm --set-metadata=\"version=\\\"$VERSION\\\"\")\n#\n#   arch:    moltis-${VERSION}-1-${ARCH}.pkg.tar.zst\n#            (manual tar with pkgver = ${VERSION}-1)\n#\n#   binary:  moltis-${VERSION}-${TARGET}.tar.gz\n#            (manual tar)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nVERSION=\"20260101.01\"  # test version\n\necho \"Checking install.sh package filename patterns...\"\necho \"  test version: $VERSION\"\necho \"\"\n\n# --- .deb checks ---\nfor arch_pair in \"x86_64:amd64\" \"aarch64:arm64\"; do\n  raw_arch=\"${arch_pair%%:*}\"\n  deb_arch=\"${arch_pair##*:}\"\n  expected=\"moltis_${VERSION}_${deb_arch}.deb\"\n\n  url=$(extract_filename install_deb \"$raw_arch\" \"$VERSION\")\n  filename=$(basename \"$url\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"\")\n\n  CHECKS=$((CHECKS + 1))\n  if [[ \"$filename\" == \"$expected\" ]]; then\n    pass \"deb ($deb_arch): $filename\"\n  else\n    fail \"deb ($deb_arch): expected '$expected', got '$filename'\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# --- .rpm checks ---\nfor arch in \"x86_64\" \"aarch64\"; do\n  expected=\"moltis-${VERSION}-1.${arch}.rpm\"\n\n  url=$(extract_filename install_rpm \"$arch\" \"$VERSION\")\n  filename=$(basename \"$url\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"\")\n\n  CHECKS=$((CHECKS + 1))\n  if [[ \"$filename\" == \"$expected\" ]]; then\n    pass \"rpm ($arch): $filename\"\n  else\n    fail \"rpm ($arch): expected '$expected', got '$filename'\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# --- .pkg.tar.zst (Arch) checks ---\nfor arch in \"x86_64\" \"aarch64\"; do\n  expected=\"moltis-${VERSION}-1-${arch}.pkg.tar.zst\"\n\n  url=$(extract_filename install_arch \"$arch\" \"$VERSION\")\n  filename=$(basename \"$url\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"\")\n\n  CHECKS=$((CHECKS + 1))\n  if [[ \"$filename\" == \"$expected\" ]]; then\n    pass \"arch ($arch): $filename\"\n  else\n    fail \"arch ($arch): expected '$expected', got '$filename'\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# --- binary tarball checks ---\nfor os_arch in \"linux:x86_64:x86_64-unknown-linux-gnu\" \"linux:aarch64:aarch64-unknown-linux-gnu\" \"macos:x86_64:x86_64-apple-darwin\" \"macos:aarch64:aarch64-apple-darwin\"; do\n  IFS=: read -r os arch target <<< \"$os_arch\"\n  expected=\"moltis-${VERSION}-${target}.tar.gz\"\n\n  url=$(extract_filename install_binary \"$os\" \"$arch\" \"$VERSION\")\n  filename=$(basename \"$url\" 2>/dev/null || echo \"\")\n\n  CHECKS=$((CHECKS + 1))\n  if [[ \"$filename\" == \"$expected\" ]]; then\n    pass \"binary ($target): $filename\"\n  else\n    fail \"binary ($target): expected '$expected', got '$filename'\"\n  fi\ndone\n\n# --- Release tag checks (date-based vs semver) ---\necho \"\"\necho \"Checking release_tag() version→tag mapping...\"\n\ncheck_tag() {\n  local version=\"$1\" expected_tag=\"$2\"\n  local actual_tag\n  actual_tag=$(\n    . \"$INSTALL_SH\" 2>/dev/null\n    release_tag \"$version\"\n  )\n  CHECKS=$((CHECKS + 1))\n  if [[ \"$actual_tag\" == \"$expected_tag\" ]]; then\n    pass \"release_tag('$version') = '$actual_tag'\"\n  else\n    fail \"release_tag('$version'): expected '$expected_tag', got '$actual_tag'\"\n  fi\n}\n\ncheck_tag \"20260327.05\" \"20260327.05\"   # date-based: bare tag\ncheck_tag \"0.1.3\"       \"v0.1.3\"        # semver: v-prefixed\n\n# --- Summary ---\necho \"\"\nif [[ \"$FAILURES\" -gt 0 ]]; then\n  echo \"FAILED: $FAILURES of $CHECKS checks failed\"\n  exit 1\nelse\n  echo \"All $CHECKS install package name checks passed\"\nfi\n"
}

> AGENT

The approach of sourcing install.sh and intercepting download/sudo is fragile across platforms. Let me take a simpler, more robust approach — just grep the install script for the filename patterns directly.

> TOOL

tool_use Write
id: toolu_014ztgS5b3hZCpFf5ZJ8MV9L
```json
{
  "file_path": "/Users/penso/tmp/molt/moltis/scripts/check-install-package-names.sh",
  "content": "#!/usr/bin/env bash\n# Verifies that install.sh constructs download filenames matching the patterns\n# produced by the release workflow for every package type.\n#\n# This catches drift between the installer and CI packaging (e.g. the -1\n# revision suffix that cargo-deb does not emit).\n#\n# Expected filename patterns (from .github/workflows/release.yml):\n#\n#   deb:     moltis_${VERSION}_${ARCH}.deb\n#            (cargo deb --deb-version \"$VERSION\" — no revision suffix)\n#\n#   rpm:     moltis-${VERSION}-1.${ARCH}.rpm\n#            (cargo generate-rpm adds -1 release)\n#\n#   arch:    moltis-${VERSION}-1-${ARCH}.pkg.tar.zst\n#            (manual tar with pkgver = ${VERSION}-1)\n#\n#   binary:  moltis-${VERSION}-${TARGET}.tar.gz\n#            (manual tar)\n\nset -euo pipefail\n\nREPO_ROOT=\"$(cd \"$(dirname \"${BASH_SOURCE[0]}\")/..\" && pwd)\"\nINSTALL_SH=\"$REPO_ROOT/install.sh\"\nRELEASE_YML=\"$REPO_ROOT/.github/workflows/release.yml\"\n\nif [[ ! -f \"$INSTALL_SH\" ]]; then\n  echo \"FAIL: install.sh not found at $INSTALL_SH\" >&2\n  exit 1\nfi\n\nFAILURES=0\nCHECKS=0\n\nfail() {\n  echo \"FAIL: $1\" >&2\n  FAILURES=$((FAILURES + 1))\n}\n\npass() {\n  echo \"  ok: $1\"\n  CHECKS=$((CHECKS + 1))\n}\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Check 1: .deb filename must NOT have a revision suffix\n#\n# Release workflow: cargo deb --deb-version \"$MOLTIS_VERSION\"\n#   → produces moltis_${VERSION}_${ARCH}.deb (no -1)\n#\n# install.sh must use: moltis_${version}_${deb_arch}.deb\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\necho \"Checking .deb filename pattern...\"\n\ndeb_line=$(grep 'deb_file=' \"$INSTALL_SH\" | head -1)\n\nif echo \"$deb_line\" | grep -q '_${version}_${deb_arch}\\.deb'; then\n  pass \".deb filename: no revision suffix (matches cargo-deb output)\"\nelif echo \"$deb_line\" | grep -q '_${version}-[0-9]'; then\n  fail \".deb filename has revision suffix but cargo-deb --deb-version does not produce one. Line: $deb_line\"\nelse\n  fail \".deb filename pattern unrecognized. Line: $deb_line\"\nfi\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Check 2: .rpm filename must have -1 revision\n#\n# Release workflow: cargo generate-rpm --set-metadata=\"version=\\\"$VERSION\\\"\"\n#   → produces moltis-${VERSION}-1.${ARCH}.rpm\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\necho \"Checking .rpm filename pattern...\"\n\nrpm_line=$(grep 'rpm_file=' \"$INSTALL_SH\" | head -1)\n\nif echo \"$rpm_line\" | grep -q '\\-${version}-1\\.${rpm_arch}\\.rpm'; then\n  pass \".rpm filename: has -1 revision (matches cargo-generate-rpm output)\"\nelse\n  fail \".rpm filename pattern does not match expected 'moltis-\\${version}-1.\\${rpm_arch}.rpm'. Line: $rpm_line\"\nfi\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Check 3: .pkg.tar.zst (Arch) filename must have -1 revision\n#\n# Release workflow: tar ... \"moltis-${VERSION}-1-${MATRIX_ARCH}.pkg.tar.zst\"\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\necho \"Checking .pkg.tar.zst (Arch) filename pattern...\"\n\narch_line=$(grep 'pkg_file=' \"$INSTALL_SH\" | head -1)\n\nif echo \"$arch_line\" | grep -q '\\-${version}-1-${arch}\\.pkg\\.tar\\.zst'; then\n  pass \".pkg.tar.zst filename: has -1 revision (matches release workflow)\"\nelse\n  fail \".pkg.tar.zst filename does not match expected 'moltis-\\${version}-1-\\${arch}.pkg.tar.zst'. Line: $arch_line\"\nfi\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Check 4: binary tarball filename\n#\n# Release workflow: tar ... \"moltis-${VERSION}-${BUILD_TARGET}.tar.gz\"\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\necho \"Checking binary tarball filename pattern...\"\n\ntarball_line=$(grep 'tarball=' \"$INSTALL_SH\" | head -1)\n\nif echo \"$tarball_line\" | grep -q '${BINARY_NAME}-${version}-${target}\\.tar\\.gz'; then\n  pass \"binary tarball: matches release workflow pattern\"\nelse\n  fail \"binary tarball pattern does not match expected '\\${BINARY_NAME}-\\${version}-\\${target}.tar.gz'. Line: $tarball_line\"\nfi\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Check 5: release_tag() handles date-based and semver versions\n#\n# Date-based (YYYYMMDD.NN) → bare tag\n# Semver → v-prefixed\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\necho \"Checking release_tag() logic...\"\n\n# Source just the release_tag function\neval \"$(sed -n '/^release_tag()/,/^}/p' \"$INSTALL_SH\")\"\n\ntag=$(release_tag \"20260327.05\")\nCHECKS=$((CHECKS + 1))\nif [[ \"$tag\" == \"20260327.05\" ]]; then\n  pass \"release_tag('20260327.05') = '20260327.05' (date-based, bare)\"\nelse\n  fail \"release_tag('20260327.05'): expected '20260327.05', got '$tag'\"\nfi\n\ntag=$(release_tag \"0.1.3\")\nCHECKS=$((CHECKS + 1))\nif [[ \"$tag\" == \"v0.1.3\" ]]; then\n  pass \"release_tag('0.1.3') = 'v0.1.3' (semver, v-prefixed)\"\nelse\n  fail \"release_tag('0.1.3'): expected 'v0.1.3', got '$tag'\"\nfi\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Check 6: .deb arch mapping\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\necho \"Checking architecture mappings...\"\n\n# deb: x86_64→amd64, aarch64→arm64\nif grep -q 'x86_64) deb_arch=\"amd64\"' \"$INSTALL_SH\"; then\n  pass \"deb arch: x86_64 → amd64\"\nelse\n  fail \"deb arch mapping for x86_64 not found\"\nfi\n\nif grep -q 'aarch64) deb_arch=\"arm64\"' \"$INSTALL_SH\"; then\n  pass \"deb arch: aarch64 → arm64\"\nelse\n  fail \"deb arch mapping for aarch64 not found\"\nfi\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Check 7: Cross-validate against release.yml if present\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\nif [[ -f \"$RELEASE_YML\" ]]; then\n  echo \"Cross-validating against release.yml...\"\n\n  # Verify cargo-deb does NOT use --deb-revision (which would add -1)\n  if grep -q '\\-\\-deb-revision' \"$RELEASE_YML\"; then\n    fail \"release.yml uses --deb-revision which adds a suffix, but install.sh assumes no suffix\"\n  else\n    pass \"release.yml: no --deb-revision flag (consistent with install.sh)\"\n  fi\n\n  # Verify cargo-deb uses --deb-version\n  if grep -q '\\-\\-deb-version' \"$RELEASE_YML\"; then\n    pass \"release.yml: uses --deb-version for .deb naming\"\n  else\n    fail \"release.yml: --deb-version not found — .deb naming may have changed\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n# Check 8: Verify both install scripts are in sync (belt + suspenders)\n# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------\n\necho \"Checking install.sh sync...\"\n\nWEBSITE_SH=\"$REPO_ROOT/website/install.sh\"\nif [[ -f \"$WEBSITE_SH\" ]]; then\n  if cmp -s \"$INSTALL_SH\" \"$WEBSITE_SH\"; then\n    pass \"install.sh and website/install.sh are identical\"\n  else\n    fail \"install.sh and website/install.sh have diverged — run ./scripts/sync-website-install.sh\"\n  fi\nfi\n\n# --- Summary ---\necho \"\"\nif [[ \"$FAILURES\" -gt 0 ]]; then\n  echo \"FAILED: $FAILURES of $CHECKS checks failed\"\n  exit 1\nelse\n  echo \"All $CHECKS install package name checks passed\"\nfi\n"
}
```

> AGENT

Pushed. Here's what was done:

**Fix:** Removed the spurious `-1` revision suffix from the `.deb` filename in both `install.sh` and `website/install.sh` (line 347). The script was building `moltis_20260327.05-1_arm64.deb` but `cargo deb --deb-version` produces `moltis_20260327.05_arm64.deb`.

**Tests:** Created `scripts/check-install-package-names.sh` with 13 checks that validate:
- `.deb` filename has no revision suffix (matches `cargo-deb` output)
- `.rpm` filename has `-1` revision (matches `cargo-generate-rpm` output)
- `.pkg.tar.zst` filename has `-1` revision (matches Arch workflow)
- Binary tarball pattern matches release workflow
- `release_tag()` handles both date-based and semver versions
- Architecture mappings (x86_64→amd64, aarch64→arm64)
- Cross-validates against `release.yml` (no `--deb-revision` flag, uses `--deb-version`)
- Both install scripts stay in sync

The check runs in both **CI** (`.github/workflows/ci.yml`) and **local-validate.sh** (as a parallel fast check).

```
     1→#!/usr/bin/env bash
     2→
     3→set -euo pipefail
     4→
     5→usage() {
     6→  cat <<'EOF'
     7→Usage: ./scripts/prepare-release.sh [version]
     8→
     9→Examples:
    10→  ./scripts/prepare-release.sh              # auto: YYYYMMDD.NN from today + next seq
    11→  ./scripts/prepare-release.sh 20260311.01  # explicit version
    12→
    13→Version format: YYYYMMDD.NN (date + two-digit daily sequence number).
    14→
    15→This command:
    16→1) generates release notes for <version> via git-cliff from unreleased commits,
    17→2) keeps a fresh empty [Unreleased] section at the top of CHANGELOG.md,
    18→3) syncs Cargo.lock via cargo fetch.
    19→EOF
    20→}
    21→
    22→if [[ $# -gt 1 ]]; then
    23→  usage
    24→  exit 1
    25→fi
    26→
    27→repo_root="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")/.." && pwd)"
    28→cd "$repo_root"
    29→
    30→if ! command -v git-cliff >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    31→  echo "git-cliff is required. Install it first (for example: cargo install git-cliff --locked)." >&2
    32→  exit 1
    33→fi
    34→
    35→if [[ ! -f Cargo.toml || ! -f CHANGELOG.md || ! -f cliff.toml ]]; then
    36→  echo "run this script from the repository root (Cargo.toml, CHANGELOG.md, cliff.toml required)" >&2
    37→  exit 1
    38→fi
    39→
    40→# Compute or validate version
    41→if [[ $# -eq 1 ]]; then
    42→  new_version="$1"
    43→  if ! [[ "$new_version" =~ ^[0-9]{8}\.[0-9]{1,2}$ ]]; then
    44→    echo "invalid version: '$new_version' (expected YYYYMMDD.NN)" >&2
    45→    exit 1
    46→  fi
    47→else
    48→  # Auto-compute: today's date + next sequence number
    49→  today="$(date -u +%Y%m%d)"
    50→  # Find highest existing seq for today's tags
    51→  max_seq=0
    52→  for tag in $(git tag -l "${today}.*" 2>/dev/null); do
    53→    seq="${tag#"${today}."}"
    54→    if [[ "$seq" =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]] && [[ "10#$seq" -gt "10#$max_seq" ]]; then
    55→      max_seq="$seq"
    56→    fi
    57→  done
    58→  next_seq=$((10#$max_seq + 1))
    59→  new_version="$(printf '%s.%02d' "$today" "$next_seq")"
    60→  echo "auto-computed version: $new_version"
    61→fi
    62→
    63→release_date="$(echo "$new_version" | sed 's/^\([0-9]\{4\}\)\([0-9]\{2\}\)\([0-9]\{2\}\)\..*/\1-\2-\3/')"
    64→
    65→if rg -q "^## \\[$new_version\\]" CHANGELOG.md; then
    66→  echo "CHANGELOG.md already contains version $new_version" >&2
    67→  exit 1
    68→fi
    69→
    70→release_section_tmp="$(mktemp)"
    71→if ! git-cliff \
    72→  --config cliff.toml \
    73→  --unreleased \
    74→  --tag "$new_version" \
    75→  --strip all \
    76→  > "$release_section_tmp"; then
    77→  rm -f "$release_section_tmp"
    78→  echo "failed to generate release notes via git-cliff" >&2
    79→  exit 1
    80→fi
    81→
    82→dated_release_section_tmp="$(mktemp)"
    83→if ! awk -v version="$new_version" -v date="$release_date" '
    84→BEGIN {
    85→  replaced = 0
    86→}
    87→{
    88→  if (replaced == 0 && $0 ~ ("^## \\[" version "\\] - [0-9]{4}-[0-9]{2}-[0-9]{2}$")) {
    89→    print "## [" version "] - " date
    90→    replaced = 1
    91→    next
    92→  }
    93→  print
    94→}
    95→END {
    96→  if (replaced == 0) {
    97→    exit 13
    98→  }
    99→}
   100→' "$release_section_tmp" > "$dated_release_section_tmp"; then
   101→  rc=$?
   102→  rm -f "$release_section_tmp" "$dated_release_section_tmp"
   103→  if [[ "$rc" -eq 13 ]]; then
   104→    echo "git-cliff output did not contain expected release header for version $new_version" >&2
   105→  fi
   106→  exit 1
   107→fi
   108→mv "$dated_release_section_tmp" "$release_section_tmp"
   109→
   110→changelog_tmp="$(mktemp)"
   111→if ! awk -v release_section_file="$release_section_tmp" '
   112→function print_empty_unreleased() {
   113→  print "## [Unreleased]"
   114→  print ""
   115→  print "### Added"
   116→  print ""
   117→  print "### Changed"
   118→  print ""
   119→  print "### Deprecated"
   120→  print ""
   121→  print "### Removed"
   122→  print ""
   123→  print "### Fixed"
   124→  print ""
   125→  print "### Security"
   126→}
   127→function print_release_section(   line) {
   128→  while ((getline line < release_section_file) > 0) {
   129→    print line
   130→  }
   131→  close(release_section_file)
   132→}
   133→BEGIN {
   134→  replaced = 0
   135→  skipping_old_unreleased = 0
   136→}
   137→{
   138→  if (replaced == 0 && $0 == "## [Unreleased]") {
   139→    print_empty_unreleased()
   140→    print ""
   141→    print_release_section()
   142→    print ""
   143→    replaced = 1
   144→    skipping_old_unreleased = 1
   145→    next
   146→  }
   147→  if (skipping_old_unreleased == 1) {
   148→    if ($0 ~ /^## \[[0-9]/) {
   149→      skipping_old_unreleased = 0
   150→      print
   151→    }
   152→    next
   153→  }
   154→  print
   155→}
   156→END {
   157→  if (replaced == 0) {
   158→    exit 12
   159→  }
   160→}
   161→' CHANGELOG.md > "$changelog_tmp"; then
   162→  rc=$?
   163→  rm -f "$release_section_tmp" "$changelog_tmp"
   164→  if [[ "$rc" -eq 12 ]]; then
   165→    echo "failed to locate '## [Unreleased]' in CHANGELOG.md" >&2
   166→  fi
   167→  exit 1
   168→fi
   169→mv "$changelog_tmp" CHANGELOG.md
   170→rm -f "$release_section_tmp"
   171→
   172→cargo fetch
   173→cargo fetch --locked
   174→
   175→# Rebuild changelog HTML for the website
   176→node website/scripts/build-changelog.mjs
   177→
   178→echo "Release prep complete:"
   179→echo "  version: $new_version"
   180→echo "  date:    $release_date"
   181→
```
